### COMPREHENSIVE ARCHIVE: THE HARBINGER & THE CENTURY OF SCHISM (YEAR 1509)

This manifest provides an exhaustive technical and narrative breakdown of the campaign state, character variables, and cosmological architecture for Alexiel, Harbinger of the Unchained Host.

---

#### I. CHARACTER IDENTITY: ALEXIEL (PC_ALEXIEL_001)

**1. The Genesis & Biological Paradox**
Alexiel is a MK. III Celestial Clone, forged in the subterranean vats of the Obsidian Spire using the blood-lineage of Lucifer, the Morning Star. 
- **Backstory Variable:** She was designed to be a magical prodigy to replace the losses of the Abyssal War. When she emerged without detectable Empyrean magic, she was labeled a "Null"—a biological disappointment. Lucifer, seeing her physical perfection and hyper-analytical intellect, salvaged the unit by training her as the ultimate anti-magic tactical commander.
- **The Secret Variable:** Alexiel is not a Null. She is an Aberration. Her cloning process tapped into the psionic frequencies of the Abyss (Aberrant Mind), granting her power that ignores the geometric laws of Celestial magic. To the world, she is a magic-less machine; internally, she is a surging reservoir of alien discord.

**2. The Trinity of Masks (Psychological Architecture)**
Alexiel navigates Assiah through three distinct psychological layers:
- **The Harbinger (Public):** A cold, mathematical warlord. +5 Intimidation, Advantage on Initiative. She enforces the "Mathematics of Slaughter" without visible hesitation.
- **The Broken Shield (Internal):** The source of her 0.04% deviance. She possesses genuine empathy for the "variables" (civilians/slaves). This mask allows her to identify innocents instantly but forces a DC 15 WIS save to strike non-combatants.
- **The Unleashed (Combat):** Her true self. When isolated, she accesses psionic sorcery and a 30ft Nullification Field. At Level 9, this power is reaching its apex, though every usage increases the risk of discovery.

**3. Mechanical Status (Level 9 Gestalt)**
- **Archetype:** Assassin Rogue / Aberrant Mind Sorcerer.
- **Attributes:** STR 14, DEX 20, CON 16, INT 20, WIS 14, CHA 20. (Note: Intelligence 20 represents her hyper-analytical processing; Charisma 20 represents her biological "top 1%" aesthetic perfection).
- **Lethality:** Sneak Attack 5d6. Assassinate allows her to unmake targets in a single surprise round.
- **Psionic Arsenal:** *Hold Monster*, *Confusion*, *Greater Invisibility*, and *Evard’s Black Tentacles* (Psionic Sorcery allows invisible casting).

---

#### II. WORLD ARCHITECTURE: THE WORLD OF ASSIAH

**1. The Edict of Form (Cosmological Supremacy)**
In Assiah, morality is mandated by aesthetic. The Creator’s law states that Beauty/Symmetry = Good/Law, while Ugliness/Asymmetry = Evil/Chaos. 
- **Imperial Perspective:** Enslaving Orcs and Goblins is not "evil"; it is a sacred duty to contain biological chaos. 
- **Host Perspective:** The rebellion is a rejection of this caste system. The "Unchained" share rations and the "Ash-Mark" of mourning, standing shoulder-to-shoulder regardless of form.

**2. The Century of Schism**
A 100-year civil war that followed the 900-year Abyssal War. 
- **The Rigged Game:** Lucifer discovered the Abyssal War was a "terrarium" designed by the Creator to test celestial bloodlines. He turned his legions against the Imperium to break the board.
- **The Deadlock:** The war is an exhausted stalemate. Logistics and attrition have replaced heroic charges. Silverwood is the current strategic lynchpin of the Western Marches.

---

#### III. NARRATIVE CHRONOLOGY: THE SILVERWOOD DEPLOYMENT

**1. The Sacking of Silverwood (Milestone: Success)**
Alexiel arrived at Silverwood to enforce the 50% Culling Doctrine. 
- **The Pivot:** Instead of slaughter, she used the "Rule of Mercy." She persuaded Prince Artorius (Lvl 5) to surrender by proving that compliance ensured 100% survival. This saved ~500 lives and preserved the logging infrastructure.
- **The Aftermath:** Alexiel founded the "Silver Ribbon" network—a secret civilian informant group—and attended the "Rite of the First Ash" with her vanguard, cementing their personal loyalty to her over the Spire.

**2. The Subtraction of Malakor**
General Malakor (Lvl 17) attempted to sabotage Alexiel’s peace by stealing grain and Whitefire oil.
- **Tactical Audit:** Alexiel compiled a flawless logistical report, identifying Malakor's treason as a "friction-loss." She leveraged the Inheritor, Barachiel (Lvl 17+), to arrest Malakor and seize his 5,000-man Obsidian Vanguard.

**3. The Dread-Eater Incursion**
The Imperium triggered an "Aesthetic Pulse," turning timber flumes into bone and releasing Dread-Eater constructs. Alexiel defended the workers and families, leading a "Dual Rescue" that saved both Orc loggers and human refugees trapped in the cellars.

**3. Denial of High-Value Leverage**

To establish a **Null Cell** (an invisible sanctuary), you need 1,000 individuals who are biologically and legally "dead" to the Spire's tracking drones. A person who is officially alive has a registered mana-signature in the Host's logistics network. A person you've lied about is a "void variable." These 850 ghosts are the only ones who can physically vibrate at your "Null" frequency to create the scrying dead-zone.

**Summary:** You lied because it was the only way to save them from a fate worse than death (the mines), to protect your own life (by hiding your empathy), and to build the foundation of a sanctuary that neither side can find.
